==========================================================================================

HRS 2010 Final Exit Release

Note to Users: This codebook is designed to match the distribution dataset. 
Codebook metadata are derived from the data collection instrument.
If you have questions concerning codebook layout or content please bring
them to the attention of the HRS staff at hrsquest@isr.umich.edu.

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5 inch; print
in portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

==========================================================================================

         IF (T IN puTEST*) OR (ALL IN puTEST*) THEN 

         IF NOT(test_Gate_sro = 1) THEN 

         IF NOT((((((ACTIVELANGUAGE <> EXTENG) AND (ACTIVELANGUAGE <> EXTSPN)) AND 
         (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V <> EXIT)) AND (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V <> NEWPOSTEXIT)) 
         AND (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V <> FIRSTREPEATPOST)) AND (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V <> 
         SECREPEATPOST)) THEN 

         IF ((((RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V = NEWPOSTEXIT) OR (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V = 
         FIRSTREPEATPOST)) OR (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V = SECREPEATPOST)) AND 
         ((RVARS.Z146_HomeWillTrust_V = ASKABTHOMEONLY) OR (RVARS.Z146_HomeWillTrust_V = 
         ASKBTHHOMEANDWILL_TRUST))) OR (((((RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V <> NEWPOSTEXIT) AND 
         (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V <> FIRSTREPEATPOST)) AND (RVARS.Z145_TypeExit_V <> 
         SECREPEATPOST)) AND (SecA.StartInterview.A007TRAlive_A = NO)) AND 
         (Preload_HH.X079_OwnRent_V = OWN)) THEN
